# June2017 effect-engine key matrix

Generated by `python june2017_re/census.py`. **Do not edit by hand**: rerun it. This is the
finish line of every later phase of `JUNE2017_EFFECT_REBUILD_PLAN.md`. A parser phase is done
when its rows leave `to-implement`; `python june2017_re/census.py --summary` prints the counts.

## How to read it

- **One row is one key in one sub-table**: family, scope root, sub-path under the root, key.
  Numbered keys are folded to the June template (`START_PARTICLE%d`), and the same name in two
  sub-tables is two rows.
- **June side**: `xorstrings.tsv` (ok rows only) and the plaintext sub-table names each reader
  references, through the same `reconcile.Binary` / `reconcile.Explainer` machinery that builds
  `june2017_re/reconcile.md`. `direct` means a scope reader decrypts or references it;
  `other-func`/`nested-subtable` means a callee on the reader's path does.
- **March side**: X2Lib's parser TUs (`X2DamageEffect.cpp`, `X2EffectSet.cpp`, `X2DamageManager.cpp`, `X2BuffFactor.cpp`, `X2BuffBehaviorTemplet.cpp`, `X2BuffFinalizerTemplet.cpp`, `X2BuffTempletManager.cpp`) preprocessed with `cl /P` under the X2Lib
  `US_SERVICE` defines plus `UNICODE`. A read counts only if it survives the preprocessor, and
  only inside the scope's March function at the same sub-table path (tracked through
  `BeginTable`/`EndTable`). Stringized keys (`CASE_BEHAVIOR_FACTOR` → `STRINGIZEA2(BBT_STUN)`)
  arrive as literals. The "leads" columns (quoted literal in raw source, bare identifier count)
  are leads only: a raw hit that is not in the preprocessed text is compiled out.
- **Status**, exactly one per row:
  - `implemented`: June reads it and March reads it at the same path.
  - `to-implement`: June reads it and March does not. `phase` is the owner.
  - `unsupported`: as to-implement, but it belongs to a system this build lacks (see
    *Absent systems*). Parse, store, log once; no behaviour (plan rule 5).
  - `binary-only`: a June reader asks for it and no in-scope June data uses it. Still
    implemented under plan rule 3; `phase` is the owner.
  - `june-ignores`: the data uses it and June never reads it (typo, dead data, removed,
    positional entry, or under a sub-table June never opens). Nothing to implement.
    **This fifth status is not in the plan's list of four**: without it, June's own dead
    data would have to be mislabelled `to-implement`.
- **Phases** follow the plan: EffectSet templet 2; `EFFECT%d` and its sub-blocks 3; CEffect top
  level 5; create/die/hit damage effects and die conditions 6a; the other sub-loaders 6b;
  DamageData 8a (keys March reads: semantics), 8b (new, system present), 8c (absent system).
  `unassigned`: no phase of the plan owns it (nested `BUFF_FACTOR`, `Elsword_DamageData.lua`,
  `DamageSound`). See *Open questions*.
- **semantics** (implemented rows with a decrypt site): June's getter call is read at the
  decrypt site (`call getter; test al,al; jne; <default store>`; getter types in `GETTERS`) and
  compared with March's expanded `GetValue` statement. `same`, `differ(...)`, or `unchecked(...)`
  when either side is not in the standard shape. `post:` lists arithmetic June does on the
  value right after reading it: a lead for the owning phase, not a verdict.

## Checks

- PASS every `june-ignores` row has a certain reason (no `name-collision` / `plain-only` / `decoder-miss?` / unrooted sub-table)
- PASS every open row has an owning phase
- PASS every code-built key is covered by a declared row
- PASS every March function in the scope table was found in its preprocessed TU, except the declared compiled-out ones: `CX2EffectSet::AddEffectSet_Description_LUA` (#ifdef EFFECT_TOOL, X2EffectSet.cpp:3380)
- PASS every row has exactly one status
- PASS every hand-written `RUNTIME` citation names a row of the matrix
- PASS every hand-verified `HAND_SEMANTICS` verdict (Phase 8a) names a March-read row
- PASS every key March reads in `ES.templet`, `ES.EFFECT`, `ES.CAMERA_SHAKE`, `ES.SOUND`, at every sub-path, has a runtime citation (Phase 4b exit; `ES.Description` is EFFECT_TOOL-only)
- PASS no runtime citation is a phase placeholder (`4b@VA`)
- PASS every runtime citation is transcribed: no `stage N` state left (Phase 4b done)
- PASS every hand-written `ES_LEVELS` range of 321e48e0 starts at a BeginTable on its own name and ends at its re-test / EndTable
- PASS every phase-8c `DE.DAMAGE_DATA` row (absent or gated-off system) has a March parse site: parsed, stored and logged under plan rule 5 (Phase 8c exit)
- PASS KTDGXMeshPlayer: 0 keys `to-implement` (Phase 10 exit)
- PASS KTDGXMeshPlayer: every `unsupported` key is still parsed by March (plan rule 5)
- PASS KTDGXMeshPlayer: no June data key is one June's own parser rejects
- PASS KTDGXMeshPlayer: every hand-written runtime citation names a key March now reads
- PASS KTDGParticleSystem: 0 keys `to-implement` (Phase 10 exit)
- PASS KTDGParticleSystem: every `unsupported` key is still parsed by March (plan rule 5)
- PASS KTDGParticleSystem: no June data key is one June's own parser rejects
- PASS KTDGParticleSystem: every hand-written runtime citation names a key March now reads

### March reads it, June does not

Rows June never reads (`june-ignores`) at a path where March does read them under `US_SERVICE`.
June dropped these, so March behaviour here is not June's. The owning phase decides: keep
(a local feature) or put it behind the June flag.

| phase | scope | key | why June ignores it | March site |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 8a | `DE.DAMAGE_DATA` (top) | `BUFF_FACTOR_ID_MEMO` | as BUFF_FACTOR_RELATIVE_MEMO_ID (March X2DamageManager.cpp:3526, same #ifdef) | X2DamageManager:4025 |
| 8a | `DE.DAMAGE_DATA` (top) | `BUFF_FACTOR_RELATIVE_MEMO_ID` | March reads it only under #ifdef ADD_MEMO_1ST_CLASS (X2DamageManager.cpp:3511). June's counterpart 32630d20 decrypts the non-memo neighbours (BUFF_FACTOR_RELATIVE_SKILL_ID, RELATIV | X2DamageManager:4010 |

Implemented rows whose June read is followed by arithmetic before the next key. Their
verdict is `unchecked` until the owning phase reads the site:

- 3 `ES.EFFECT` (top) `LIMIT_DISTANCE`: unchecked(post-processing fmul@321ea1d3)
- 6b `DE.SCREEN` (top) `COLOR_B`: unchecked(post-processing imul@31f65d69)

- 6b `DE.SOUND` (top) `THREE_DIMENSIONAL_SOUND`: unchecked(post-processing imul@31f747b8)

## Runtime (Phase 4a on)

What June does with a key at run time, and where. The table is written by hand (`RUNTIME` in
`census.py`) from the June disassembly. It is kept apart from the parse-time `semantics` column, so
a getter verdict and a behaviour verdict never overwrite each other. `state` is `done`
(transcribed), `stage N` (owned, not yet written), `deferred`, or `-` (nothing to write).
`outside-effectset` means only another subsystem reads the key; the branch names it.
The full spec is in the plan, Phases 4a and 4b.

192 keys cited: - 1, new 81, not-creation 1, outside-effectset 3, same 78, semantics-differ 22, unsupported 6.

## Absent systems

Searched in the preprocessed parser TUs (compiled in under `US_SERVICE`), in raw `X2Lib/`,
`KTDXLIB/` and `KncWX2Server/Common/` (packets, shared enums) sources, and for the `#if` flag that encloses each raw hit (resolved by a `cl /P`
probe that includes X2Lib's `stdafx.h`). Terms are whole identifiers, listed below the table.

- `absent`: no identifier anywhere in `X2Lib/` or `KTDXLIB/`.
- `gated-off`: code exists, but every enclosing flag is off under `US_SERVICE`.
- `present`: compiled into one of the parser TUs.
- `present-elsewhere`: compiled in (every enclosing flag ON), outside the parser TUs. The
  system exists, so its keys are ordinary work, not `unsupported`.
- `source-only`: code exists, but the enclosing flags disagree or the probe could not settle one.

Only `absent` and `gated-off` make a key `unsupported` (plan Phase 8c: confirm the system is
absent before logging it as such). Hits inside a `SERV_IRUHADEV_JUNE_*` block are the rebuild's own
plan-rule-5 parse sites (parse, store, log): they are counted in the last column and are not
evidence. An unsupported row that March parses that way stays `unsupported`, with its March site.

`march2014/data036/Enum.src.lua` + `DungeonEnum.src.lua`. `reader` is the phase that owns the
key(s) using it: an enum member with no reader is not added (Phase 11 owns the data change).

## Open questions for the user

No phase of the plan owns these scopes. Their rows are `unassigned` rather than folded into
a neighbouring phase:

- nested `DAMAGE_DATA/BUFF_FACTOR/...` (the buff parser): 5 open (implemented 137, to-implement 4, unsupported 1).
- `ES.Description` (EffectSet_Description (effect-tool only)): 3 open (to-implement 3); March reads it only under `#ifdef EFFECT_TOOL` (X2EffectSet.cpp:3380), compiled out of the client.
- `InsertDamageData` (InsertDamageData_LUA (Elsword_DamageData.lua)): 9 open (to-implement 9); March has no reader for `Elsword_DamageData.lua` at all; June's reader 32621080 is a candidate.
- `DamageSound` (DamageSound): 0 open (implemented 10); March reads it (`ParseDamageSoundBlock`); June's reader 32610ff0 is a candidate.

## `.txt` templet parsers: KTDGXMeshPlayer and KTDGParticleSystem (Phase 10)

Generated by the same run (`census.py`; `census.py --meshplayer` prints only this part). These
formats are tokenizers, not Lua, and their keywords are **plaintext** in the June binary.

- **June**: each parser chain in `funcmap.tsv` (mesh player: tokenizer `31c85840`, block
  `31c8ae10`, events `31c85350`; particles: `31cf2250`, `31cfa4a0`, `31cf1e80`). Each `Is*()`
  helper the block calls pushes one keyword, which is a property June dispatches. A key counts
  only if the tokenizer knows it **and** it is dispatched. The June site is the helper call in
  the block, or the name push in the event factory.
- **March**: `KTDXLIB/KTDGXMeshPlayer.cpp` and `KTDGParticleSystem.cpp`, `cl /P` under KTDXLIB's
  own `US_SERVICE` defines and include dirs. The same two conditions on the surviving source,
  with each `Is*()` helper looked up in its own parser's token class.
- **Data**: June's `data036_kom/*XMeshPlayer*.txt` / `*Particle*.txt`, tokenized the way March's
  `Tokenize` does. `June uses` counts occurrences; `March uses` counts the live March copies
  (`kom_contents/data036_kom`, `datai015_kom`).
- **Why a to-implement key matters more here than in Lua**: March's tokenizer throws on a key it
  does not know, and the rest of that `.txt` file is never loaded.
- **Not compared**: store semantics of the particle block's shared keys (key sets only). The
  mesh player's numeric branch was compared by hand (`funcmap.tsv` `31c8ae10`).
- `march-only`: March reads it, June does not (not a gap). `june-ignores`: June data uses it and
  June's own parser rejects it. A word both tokenizers know and neither dispatches, with no data
  use, gets no row (`TEXTURECHANGE`: tokenized as an event name, no event class in either build).

**Counts**: KTDGXMeshPlayer: to-implement 0, unsupported 1, implemented 64, march-only 1, june-ignores 0; KTDGParticleSystem: to-implement 0, unsupported 1, implemented 60, march-only 0, june-ignores 0.

- PASS KTDGXMeshPlayer: 0 keys `to-implement` (Phase 10 exit)
- PASS KTDGXMeshPlayer: every `unsupported` key is still parsed by March (plan rule 5)
- PASS KTDGXMeshPlayer: no June data key is one June's own parser rejects
- PASS KTDGXMeshPlayer: every hand-written runtime citation names a key March now reads
- PASS KTDGParticleSystem: 0 keys `to-implement` (Phase 10 exit)
- PASS KTDGParticleSystem: every `unsupported` key is still parsed by March (plan rule 5)
- PASS KTDGParticleSystem: no June data key is one June's own parser rejects
- PASS KTDGParticleSystem: every hand-written runtime citation names a key March now reads
